        SELECT `y3`.`report_id`, `y3`.`absent`, `y3`.`attendanceRate`, `y3`.`average_absent`, `y3`.`average_late`, `y3`.`average_leave`, `y3`.`average_present`, `y3`.`average_undertime`, `y3`.`lastAttendanceIdProcessed`, `y3`.`late`, `y3`.`leave_count`, `y3`.`present`, `y3`.`underTime`, `y3`.`year`, 1 AS `Key`
        FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y3`
        WHERE `y3`.`year` = @__year_0
    ) AS `t3`
    WHERE `t`.`Key` = `t3`.`Key`
    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `t4`.`year`
    FROM (
        SELECT `y4`.`report_id`, `y4`.`absent`, `y4`.`attendanceRate`, `y4`.`average_absent`, `y4`.`average_late`, `y4`.`average_leave`, `y4`.`average_present`, `y4`.`average_undertime`, `y4`.`lastAttendanceIdProcessed`, `y4`.`late`, `y4`.`leave_count`, `y4`.`present`, `y4`.`underTime`, `y4`.`year`, 1 AS `Key`
        FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y4`
        WHERE `y4`.`year` = @__year_0
    ) AS `t4`
    WHERE `t`.`Key` = `t4`.`Key`
    LIMIT 1)
FROM (
    SELECT 1 AS `Key`
    FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y`
    WHERE `y`.`year` = @__year_0
) AS `t`
GROUP BY `t`.`Key`
LIMIT 1

SELECT `p`.`appointment_id` AS `Id`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `d0`.`doctor_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p3`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (
    SELECT `d4`.`status`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d4` ON `p4`.`appointment_id` = `d4`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p4`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`
WHERE (EXTRACT(month FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `p`.`appointment_id`

SELECT `p`.`appointment_id` AS `Id`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `d0`.`doctor_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p3`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (
    SELECT `d4`.`status`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d4` ON `p4`.`appointment_id` = `d4`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p4`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`
WHERE (EXTRACT(month FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `p`.`appointment_id`

    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p3`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (
    SELECT `d4`.`status`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d4` ON `p4`.`appointment_id` = `d4`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p4`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`

    FROM `pharmacy_stock_batches` AS `p1`
    WHERE `p0`.`med_id` = `p1`.`med_id`) AS `current_stock`, EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM `pharmacy_stock_batches` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p0`.`med_id` = `p2`.`med_id`) AND ((`p2`.`expiry_date` >= @__date_5) AND (`p2`.`expiry_date` <= @__next30Days_6))) AS `expiring_within_30_days`, FALSE AS `shortage_occured`
FROM `pharmacy_prescription_items` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `pharmacy_inventory` AS `p0` ON `p`.`med_id` = `p0`.`med_id`
WHERE (`p`.`dispensed_date` >= @__lastMonthStart_0) AND (`p`.`dispensed_date` < @__lastMonthEnd_1)
GROUP BY `p0`.`med_id`

    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Present' THEN 1
END) AS `present`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Absent' THEN 1
END) AS `absent`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Undertime' THEN 1
END) AS `underTime`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'Late' THEN 1
END) AS `late`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `t`.`status` = 'On Leave' THEN 1

    SELECT `h`.`status`, 1 AS `Key`
